# Requirements coverage matrix

Consolidated 2026-10-29. This is exhaustive source-clause traceability, not a declaration of feature completeness. Each nonempty paragraph/list block/table/code block in both sanitized source documents has one stable clause ID and one row. Context and repeated definition-of-done/process clauses are intentionally retained alongside functional requirements so no source passage silently disappears. Read the linked full clause, not only its shortened label.

Implementation statuses are conservative area-level assessments: code presence does not prove every clause is implemented. Missing/unverified details remain mandatory. Phase 0A baseline/audit evidence is recorded in verification.md and the prioritized backlog is backlog.md; no product acceptance is inferred. Specific contradictions/gaps are C01-C08 in [decisions](decisions.md); actual evidence/failure names are in [verification](verification.md). Master pilot keep-alive instructions are superseded by amendment; original one-shot process is superseded by the latest incremental instructions. Paid production work remains gated. Private identities are substituted, not public acceptance fixtures.

Phase 0B updates only the specific clauses with passing backend evidence below. The complete 60-case PostgreSQL run resolves the six baseline failures; unchanged area-level labels and all UI/deployed acceptance gaps remain conservative. See [current verification](verification.md) and [decisions](decisions.md) for unresolved policies.
